SELECT o.grp, SUM(o.val) AS total, (SELECT MAX(i.val) FROM split_agg_combo.t2 AS i WHERE i.grp=o.grp) AS peer_max FROM split_agg_combo.t AS o GROUP BY o.grp HAVING SUM(o.val) > (SELECT COALESCE(MIN(i.val),0) FROM split_agg_combo.t2 AS i WHERE i.grp=o.grp) ORDER BY o.grp;
SELECT d.grp,d.total FROM (SELECT grp,SUM(val) AS total,COUNT(DISTINCT id) AS distinct_ids FROM split_agg_combo.t GROUP BY grp WITH ROLLUP) AS d WHERE d.grp IS NOT NULL AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM split_agg_combo.t2 AS i WHERE i.grp=d.grp AND i.val<d.total) ORDER BY d.grp;
SELECT (SELECT SUM(x.total) FROM (SELECT grp,SUM(val) AS total FROM split_agg_combo.t GROUP BY grp) AS x WHERE x.total>(SELECT AVG(val) FROM split_agg_combo.t2)) AS filtered_total;
SELECT grp,MAX(rn) AS max_rn,SUM(MAX(rn)) OVER (ORDER BY grp) AS cumulative_max FROM (SELECT grp,ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY grp ORDER BY val,id) AS rn FROM split_agg_combo.t) AS ranked GROUP BY grp HAVING MAX(rn)>1 ORDER BY grp;
WITH ranked AS (SELECT id,grp,val,DENSE_RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY grp ORDER BY val DESC) AS rnk FROM split_agg_combo.t) SELECT grp,MAX(rnk) AS depth,SUM(val) AS total FROM ranked GROUP BY grp HAVING MAX(rnk)>=1 ORDER BY grp;
SELECT o.id,o.grp,ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY o.grp ORDER BY (SELECT MAX(i.val) FROM split_agg_combo.t2 AS i WHERE i.grp=o.grp),o.id) AS rn FROM split_agg_combo.t AS o ORDER BY o.grp,rn;
SELECT grp,AVG(val) AS avg_val,RANK() OVER (ORDER BY AVG(val)) AS avg_rank,SUM(COUNT(*)) OVER () AS all_rows FROM split_agg_combo.t GROUP BY grp HAVING COUNT(*)>0 ORDER BY grp;
SELECT o.grp, COUNT(*) AS cnt FROM t AS o GROUP BY o.grp HAVING COUNT(*) >= (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM t2 AS i WHERE i.grp = o.grp AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM t AS deep WHERE deep.id = i.id AND deep.val >= o.grp));
SELECT d.grp, d.total FROM (SELECT grp, SUM(id) AS total FROM t GROUP BY grp HAVING SUM(id) > 0) AS d WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM t2 AS i WHERE i.grp = d.grp AND i.id < d.total) ORDER BY d.grp;
SELECT o.id, (SELECT MAX(i.id) FROM t2 AS i WHERE i.grp = o.grp AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM t AS deep WHERE deep.grp = i.grp AND deep.id < o.id)) AS nested_max FROM t AS o ORDER BY o.id;
SELECT o.id,CASE WHEN EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM split_cross84.inner_t AS i WHERE i.grp=o.grp AND i.val>(SELECT AVG(d.val) FROM split_cross84.inner_t AS d WHERE d.grp=o.grp)) THEN COALESCE((SELECT MAX(m.val) FROM split_cross84.inner_t AS m WHERE m.grp=o.grp),0) ELSE 0 END AS score,agg.total FROM split_cross84.outer_t AS o LEFT JOIN (SELECT grp,SUM(val) AS total FROM split_cross84.inner_t GROUP BY grp HAVING SUM(val)>(SELECT AVG(x.val) FROM split_cross84.inner_t AS x)) AS agg ON agg.grp=o.grp AND agg.total>(SELECT COALESCE(MIN(y.val),0) FROM split_cross84.inner_t AS y WHERE y.grp=o.grp) WHERE o.id IN (SELECT i.id FROM split_cross84.inner_t AS i WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM split_cross84.outer_t AS deep WHERE deep.id=i.id AND deep.val<i.val)) ORDER BY CASE WHEN agg.total IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END,score DESC LIMIT 0,10;

SELECT a.id AS a_id, b.id AS b_id, c.id AS c_id
FROM a LEFT JOIN (b, c)
  ON a.k=b.k AND b.k=c.k
ORDER BY a.id, b.id, c.id;
SELECT a.id AS a_id, b.id AS b_id, c.id AS c_id
FROM (a, b) LEFT JOIN c
  ON b.k=c.k
ORDER BY a.id, b.id, c.id;
SELECT a.id AS a_id, b.id AS b_id, c.id AS c_id, d.id AS d_id
FROM (a, b) LEFT JOIN (c, d)
  ON b.k=c.k AND c.k=d.k
ORDER BY a.id, b.id, c.id, d.id;
SELECT a.id AS a_id, b.id AS b_id, v.id AS v_id
FROM a INNER JOIN (b LEFT JOIN v ON b.k=v.k)
ORDER BY a.id, b.id, v.id;
SELECT p.id AS p_id, c.id AS c_id, q.id AS q_id, b.id AS b_id
FROM a AS p
LEFT JOIN (c JOIN a AS q)
  ON c.k=q.k AND p.k=c.k
LEFT JOIN b
  ON c.k=b.k
ORDER BY p.id, c.id, q.id, b.id;
SELECT big.*, dt.*
FROM join_truth_big AS big
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT id AS dt_id,
           id IS NULL AS nul,
           id IS NOT NULL AS nnul,
           id IS TRUE AS t,
           id IS NOT TRUE AS nt,
           id IS FALSE AS f,
           id IS NOT FALSE AS nf,
           id IS UNKNOWN AS u,
           id IS NOT UNKNOWN AS nu
    FROM join_truth_small
) AS dt ON big.id = dt.dt_id;

           links.ionumber AS ionumber2,
           attrs.ioattribute
    FROM join_using_2 AS links
    JOIN join_using_3 AS attrs USING (ionumber)
) AS joined USING (adslot);

SELECT o.id, d.max_v,
       (SELECT SUM(i.v)
          FROM t2 AS i
         WHERE i.grp = o.grp
         GROUP BY i.grp
        HAVING SUM(i.v) > 0
         ORDER BY SUM(i.v) DESC
         LIMIT 1) AS s
  FROM t1 AS o
  LEFT JOIN (SELECT grp, MAX(v) AS max_v FROM t2 GROUP BY grp) AS d
    ON d.grp = o.grp
 WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM t2 AS e WHERE e.id = o.id)
 ORDER BY o.id
 LIMIT 5;
SELECT o.id FROM t1 AS o
WHERE EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM DUAL
    WHERE o.id IS NOT NULL
    GROUP BY 1
    HAVING COUNT(*) > 0
    ORDER BY 1
    LIMIT 1
    FOR UPDATE
)
ORDER BY o.id
LIMIT 5;

[SELECT] SELECT o.id, d.max_v,
       (SELECT SUM(i.v)
          FROM t2 AS i
         WHERE i.grp = o.grp
         GROUP BY i.grp
        HAVING SUM(i.v) > 0
         ORDER BY SUM(i.v) DESC
         LIMIT 1) AS s
  FROM t1 AS o
  LEFT JOIN (SELECT grp, MAX(v) AS max_v FROM t2 GROUP BY grp) AS d
    ON d.grp = o.grp
 WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM t2 AS e WHERE e.id = o.id)
 ORDER BY o.id
 LIMIT 5;
----------
[SELECT|QUERY_LOCK] SELECT o.id FROM t1 AS o
WHERE EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM DUAL
    WHERE o.id IS NOT NULL
    GROUP BY 1
    HAVING COUNT(*) > 0
    ORDER BY 1
    LIMIT 1
    FOR UPDATE
)
ORDER BY o.id
LIMIT 5;
----------
